<html lang="zh-TW"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0"><meta name="color-scheme" content="light dark"><title>高頻單字 3500–6000｜36 dark}:root[data-theme=light]{color-scheme:light}:root[data-theme=dark]{color-scheme:dark;--bg:#0f1420;--card:#171e2e;--tx:#e8ecf5;--mu:#97a1b6;--bd:#2a3347;--pri:#818cf8;--pri2:#6366f1;--soft:#232a45;--ok:#34d399;--okb:#12382c;--no:#f87171;--nob:#43191b;--warn:#3a3218;--sh:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.4)}}*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}body{background:var(--bg);color:var(--tx);font:16px/1.6 -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI","PingFang TC","Noto Sans TC","Microsoft JhengHei",sans-serif;padding-bottom:60px}header{position:relative;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4f46e5,#7c3aed 60%,#db2777);color:#fff;padding:28px 16px 22px;text-align:center}header h1{font-size:1.7rem;letter-spacing:.04em}header p{opacity:.85;font-size:.9rem;margin-top:4px}.stats{display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-top:14px}.stat{background:rgba(255,255,255,.18);border-radius:12px;padding:6px 14px;font-size:.82rem}.stat b{font-size:1.1rem;display:block}nav{position:sticky;top:0;z-index:50;display:flex;justify-content:center;gap:6px;padding:10px;background:var(--card);border-bottom:1px solid var(--bd);overflow-x:auto}nav button{white-space:nowrap;padding:8px 16px;border:1px solid var(--bd);background:var(--card);color:var(--tx);border-radius:999px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.15s}nav button.active,nav button:hover{background:var(--pri);border-color:var(--pri);color:#fff}.container{max-width:1040px;margin:20px auto;padding:0 14px}.tab{display:none}.tab.active{display:block;animation:fade .25s}@keyframes fade{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px)}to{opacity:1}}.panel{background:var(--card);border-radius:16px;box-shadow:var(--sh);padding:18px;margin-bottom:16px}.toolbar{display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;margin-bottom:12px}input[type=text],select{padding:10px 12px;border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:10px;background:var(--card);color:var(--tx);font-size:1rem;font-family:inherit}input[type=text],select{-webkit-appearance:none;appearance:none;-webkit-text-fill-color:var(--tx);opacity:1;font-size:16px;background-color:var(--card)}input[type=text]:disabled,select:disabled{-webkit-text-fill-color:var(--tx);color:var(--tx);opacity:.75;background-color:var(--card)}input::placeholder{color:var(--mu);-webkit-text-fill-color:var(--mu);opacity:1}select{padding-right:30px;background-image:linear-gradient(45deg,transparent 50%,var(--mu) 50%),linear-gradient(135deg,var(--mu) 50%,transparent 50%);background-position:calc(100% - 17px) 50%,calc(100% - 12px) 50%;background-size:5px 5px;background-repeat:no-repeat}input[type=text]:focus,select:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--pri)}body,.panel,.face,.opt,.cs{-webkit-text-fill-color:currentColor}.grow{flex:1;min-width:180px}.seg{display:inline-flex;border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden}.seg button{padding:8px 14px;border:0;background:var(--card);color:var(--tx);cursor:pointer;font-size:.92rem}.seg button.on{background:var(--pri);color:#fff}.btn{padding:10px 20px;border:0;border-radius:10px;background:var(--pri);color:#fff;font-size:1rem;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;transition:.15s}.btn:hover{background:var(--pri2)}.btn.ghost{background:var(--soft);color:var(--pri)}.btn.ok{background:var(--ok)}.btn.no{background:var(--no)}.btn:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:default}.chips{display:flex;gap:6px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:12px}.chip{padding:3px 11px;border-radius:999px;border:1px solid var(--bd);background:var(--card);color:var(--mu);font-size:.8rem;cursor:pointer}.chip.on{background:var(--soft);color:var(--pri);border-color:var(--pri);font-weight:600}.count{color:var(--mu);font-size:.88rem}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse}th,td{padding:11px 12px;text-align:left;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bd)}th{font-size:.82rem;color:var(--mu);font-weight:600;background:var(--soft)}tr.grp td{background:var(--soft);color:var(--pri);font-weight:700;padding:9px 12px;position:sticky;top:54px}.w{color:var(--pri);font-weight:700;cursor:pointer}.w:hover{text-decoration:underline}.pos{background:var(--soft);color:var(--pri);padding:1px 8px;border-radius:6px;font-size:.8rem;white-space:nowrap}.ut{font-size:.75rem;color:var(--mu);white-space:nowrap}.ib{background:none;border:0;cursor:pointer;font-size:1.05rem;opacity:.8}.ib:hover{opacity:1}.dot{width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;display:inline-block;background:var(--bd)}.dot.k{background:var(--ok)}.dot.u{background:#f59e0b}@media(max-width:640px){.hm{display:none}th,td{padding:9px 6px}}/* flashcard */.fcwrap{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center}.card{width:100%;max-width:520px;height:330px;perspective:1200px;cursor:pointer}.ci{position:relative;width:100%;height:100%;transition:transform .55s;transform-style:preserve-3d}.card.flip .ci{transform:rotateY(180deg)}.face{position:absolute;inset:0;backface-visibility:hidden;border-radius:20px;border:1px solid var(--bd);background:var(--card);box-shadow:var(--sh);display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:22px;text-align:center}.face.back{transform:rotateY(180deg);background:linear-gradient(160deg,var(--card),var(--soft))}.face .tag{position:absolute;top:14px;left:16px;font-size:.75rem;color:var(--mu);text-align:left;max-width:75%}.face .big{font-size:2.3rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--pri);word-break:break-word}.face .hint{position:absolute;bottom:12px;font-size:.78rem;color:var(--mu)}.face .mean{font-size:1.6rem;font-weight:700;margin:4px 0 12px}.face .ex{font-size:.98rem;text-align:left;color:var(--tx);background:var(--card);border-left:4px solid var(--pri);padding:8px 12px;border-radius:8px;width:100%}.face .ex mark{background:#fde68a;color:#111;border-radius:3px;padding:0 2px}.ex .sp{cursor:pointer}.bar{width:100%;max-width:520px;height:7px;background:var(--bd);border-radius:9px;margin:14px 0 8px;overflow:hidden}.bar i{display:block;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--pri),#db2777);transition:width .3s}.ctrl{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;margin-top:14px}/* modal */.ov{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(10,15,30,.65);backdrop-filter:blur(4px);display:none;align-items:center;justify-content:center;z-index:200;padding:14px}.ov.on{display:flex}.ov .box{width:100%;max-width:520px}/* quiz */.qh{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-bottom:10px;color:var(--mu);font-size:.9rem}.qq{font-size:1.25rem;font-weight:700;margin:6px 0 14px;line-height:1.7}.qq .bl{display:inline-block;min-width:90px;border-bottom:3px solid var(--pri);text-align:center;color:var(--pri)}.opts{display:grid;gap:9px}.opt{text-align:left;padding:12px 14px;border:1.5px solid var(--bd);border-radius:12px;background:var(--card);color:var(--tx);font-size:1rem;cursor:pointer;transition:.12s}.opt:hover:not(:disabled){border-color:var(--pri);background:var(--soft)}.opt.c{border-color:var(--ok);background:var(--okb)}.opt.x{border-color:var(--no);background:var(--nob)}.fb{margin-top:14px;padding:12px 14px;border-radius:12px;background:var(--warn);font-size:.95rem}.score{font-size:3rem;font-weight:800;text-align:center;color:var(--pri)}.rv{padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--bd);font-size:.92rem}/* cloze */.pt{font-weight:800;color:var(--pri);font-size:1.15rem;margin-bottom:10px}.bank{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:7px;background:var(--soft);padding:12px;border-radius:12px;margin-bottom:14px}.bank span{background:var(--card);border:1px solid var(--bd);border-radius:8px;padding:2px 10px;font-size:.92rem}.ptx{line-height:2.3;font-size:1.05rem}.cs{padding:2px 4px;border:1.5px solid var(--pri);border-radius:8px;background:var(--soft);color:var(--tx);font-weight:700;font-size:.95rem}.cs.ok{border-color:var(--ok);background:var(--okb)}.cs.bad{border-color:var(--no);background:var(--nob)}.ans{display:none;margin-top:12px;padding:12px 14px;border-left:4px solid #f59e0b;background:var(--warn);border-radius:8px;font-size:.92rem}.empty{text-align:center;color:var(--mu);padding:30px}/* v2 */
header{padding:34px 16px 26px}header h1{font-size:1.9rem}
.card{height:430px;max-width:560px}.bar{max-width:560px}
.face.back{justify-content:flex-start;overflow-y:auto;padding-top:40px}
.bk{width:100%;text-align:left}.bw{font-size:1.5rem;font-weight:800;color:var(--pri);margin-bottom:8px}
.sn{margin:6px 0;font-size:.98rem}
.ex2{font-size:.86rem;color:var(--mu);line-height:1.5;margin:3px 0 4px;padding-left:10px;border-left:3px solid var(--bd)}
.ex2 mark{background:#fde68a;color:#111;border-radius:3px;padding:0 2px}.ex2 .zh{display:block;color:var(--tx);opacity:.8}
.xr{display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:baseline;margin:5px 0;font-size:.86rem;flex-wrap:wrap}
.xt{flex:none;font-size:.7rem;font-weight:700;border-radius:5px;padding:1px 6px;color:#fff}
.xt-syn{background:#2563eb}.xt-ant{background:#dc2626}.xt-der{background:#059669}.xt-phr{background:#d97706}.xt-col{background:#7c3aed}.xt-idm{background:#db2777}
.xc b{color:var(--pri)}.xc{margin-right:8px}.xc i{display:block;font-style:normal;color:var(--mu);font-size:.8rem}
.yr{display:inline-block;font-size:.7rem;color:#b45309;background:#fef3c7;border-radius:6px;padding:0 6px;white-space:nowrap}.yr em{font-style:normal;opacity:.7}
:root[data-theme=dark] .yr{background:#3a3218;color:#fcd34d}
@media(prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme=light]) .yr{background:#3a3218;color:#fcd34d}}
.rf{font-size:.68rem;color:var(--mu);border:1px dashed var(--bd);border-radius:6px;padding:0 5px}
tr.part td{background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--pri),#7c3aed);color:#fff;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.04em;padding:10px 14px}
tr.grp .en{font-weight:400;color:var(--mu);font-size:.82rem;margin-left:8px}
.tp{font-size:.72rem;background:var(--card);color:var(--pri);border:1px solid var(--pri);border-radius:999px;padding:0 8px;margin-left:8px;font-weight:600}
tbody tr:not(.grp):not(.part):hover td{background:var(--soft)}
td .pos{display:inline-block;margin:1px 0}
.pl{align-self:center;font-size:.78rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--mu);margin:0 4px 0 10px}
.ui{background:var(--soft);border-radius:12px;padding:12px 14px;margin-top:10px}.ui .ut2{font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:700}.ui .en{font-weight:400;color:var(--mu);font-size:.85rem}
.mb{height:6px;background:var(--bd);border-radius:9px;margin:8px 0 4px;overflow:hidden}.mb i{display:block;height:100%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--ok),var(--pri))}.ui small{color:var(--mu)}
.tg{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;color:var(--mu);font-size:.9rem;cursor:pointer}
footer{text-align:center;color:var(--mu);font-size:.8rem;padding:22px 12px 0}
@media(max-width:640px){header h1{font-size:1.5rem}.card{height:470px}}
@media print{header,nav,.toolbar,.chips,.ib,#themeBtn,.mb,footer{display:none!important}.tab{display:none!important}#list{display:block!important}body{background:#fff;color:#000}.panel{box-shadow:none}tr.part td,tr.grp td{position:static;background:#eee!important;color:#000!important}}

ghost" id="fcShuf">🔀 洗牌</button>    <button class="btn ghost" id="fcFlipDir">正面：英文</button>  </div>  <div class="fcwrap">    <div class="card" id="card"><div class="ci">      <div class="face" <button class="btn ghost" id="fcNext">下一張 ▶</button>    </div>    <p class="count" style="margin-top:12px">快捷鍵：空白鍵翻面・← → 切換・K 已熟悉・U 還不熟・S 發音</p>  </div></section><section id="quiz" class="tab">  <div const uLabel=n=>{const u=UNITS[n-1];return `P${u.part}-U${u.n}`;};
const uFull=n=>{const u=UNITS[n-1];return `Part ${u.part} ・ Unit ${u.n}｜${u.zh}（${u.topic}）`;};
const XL={syn:['同','近義字'],ant:['反','反義字'],der:['衍','衍生字'],phr:['片','片語'],col:['詞','搭配詞'],idm:['慣','慣用語']};
const yrTxt=y=>y<0?(-y)+'試':''+y;
const yrHTML=v=>v.years.length?`<span class="yr" title="學測出現年度：${v.years.map(yrTxt).join('、')}">考 ${yrTxt(v.years[0])}${v.years.length>1?` <em>×${v.years.length}</em>`:''}</span>`:'';
const posHTML=v=>[...new Set(v.senses.map(s=>s[0]))].map(p=>`<span class="pos">${esc(p)}</span>`).join(' ');
const senseHTML=(v,ex=true)=>v.senses.map(s=>`<div class="sn"><span class="pos">${esc(s[0])}</span> ${esc(s[1])}${ex&&s[2]?`<div class="ex2">${hl(s[2])}<span class="zh">${esc(s[3])}</span></div>`:''}</div>`).join('');
function extraHTML(v){const g={};v.extras.forEach(x=>(g[x[0]]=g[x[0]]||[]).push(x));
  return Object.keys(XL).filter(k=>g[k]).map(k=>`<div class="xr"><span class="xt xt-${k}" title="${XL[k][1]}">${XL[k][0]}</span>${g[k].map(x=>`<span class="xc"><b>${esc(x[1])}</b> ${esc(x[2])}${x[3]?`<i>${hl(x[3])}　${esc(x[4])}</i>`:''}</span>`).join('')}</div>`).join('');}
const store = { get(k, d) { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(k)) ?? d; } catch (e) { return d; } }, set(k, v) { try { localStorage.setItem(k, JSON.stringify(v)); } catch (e) {} } };const k2i=new Map(vocabData.map(v=>[v.key,v.id]));
const ldK=n=>new Set((store.get(n,[])||[]).map(k=>k2i.get(k)).filter(i=>i!=null));
let known=ldK('hf2_known'),wrong=ldK('hf2_wrong');
const saveK=()=>{store.set('hf2_known',[...known].map(i=>vocabData[i].key));stats();};
const saveW=()=>{store.set('hf2_wrong',[...wrong].map(i=>vocabData[i].key));stats();};
function stats() { $('#s-total').textContent = vocabData.length; $('#s-known').textContent = known.size; $('#s-wrong').textContent = wrong.size; $('#wc').textContent = wrong.size; }function speak(t, r = .9) { if (!('speechSynthesis' in window)) return alert('您的瀏覽器不支援語音播放'); speechSynthesis.cancel(); const u = new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(String(t).replace(/\*/g,'')); u.lang = 'en-US'; u.rate = r; speechSynthesis.speak(u); }function hl(s){return esc(s||'').replace(/\*(.+?)\*/g,'<mark>$1</mark>');}
vocabData.sort((a, b) => (a.unit || 99) - (b.unit || 99) || a.no - b.no);vocabData.forEach((v, i) => v.id = i);/* ---------- 分頁 ---------- */$('#nav').addEventListener('click', e => {  const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b) return;  document.querySelectorAll('nav button').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('active', x === b));  document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('active', x.id === b.dataset.t));  if (b.dataset.t === 'fc') buildDeck();});/* ---------- 單元選單 ---------- */const usedUnits=[...new Set(vocabData.map(v=>v.unit))].sort((a,b)=>a-b);
function unitOptions(withAll=true){return (withAll?'<option value="all">全部單元</option>':'')+[1,2].map(p=>`<optgroup label="${POSN[p]}">`+UNITS.filter(u=>u.part===p).map(u=>`<option value="${u.id}">Unit ${u.n}｜${esc(u.zh)} (${vocabData.filter(v=>v.unit===u.id).length})</option>`).join('')+'</optgroup>').join('');}
['#fcUnit','#qUnit'].forEach(s=>$(s).innerHTML=unitOptions());
/* ---------- 1. 單字列表 ---------- */let sortMode='unit',chipUnit='all';
function renderChips(){$('#unitChips').innerHTML=`<span class="chip ${chipUnit==='all'?'on':''}" data-u="all">全部</span>`+[1,2].map(p=>`<span class="pl">Part ${p}</span>`+UNITS.filter(u=>u.part===p).map(u=>`<span class="chip ${chipUnit==u.id?'on':''}" data-u="${u.id}" title="${esc(uFull(u.id))}">U${u.n}</span>`).join('')).join('');}
$('#unitChips').addEventListener('click', e => { const c = e.target.closest('.chip'); if (!c) return; chipUnit = c.dataset.u; renderChips(); renderList(); });$('#sortSeg').addEventListener('click', e => { const b = e.target.closest('button'); if (!b) return; sortMode = b.dataset.v; document.querySelectorAll('#sortSeg button').forEach(x => x.classList.toggle('on', x === b)); renderList(); });['q', 'filt', 'showEx'].forEach(id => $('#' + id).addEventListener('input', renderList));function renderUnitInfo(){const el=$('#unitInfo');
  const a=chipUnit==='all'?vocabData:vocabData.filter(v=>v.unit==chipUnit),k=a.filter(v=>known.has(v.id)).length,r=a.filter(v=>v.ref).length,pc=a.length?k/a.length*100:0;
  const head=chipUnit==='all'?`<div class="ut2">全部 36 單元</div>`:(u=>`<div class="ut1"><span class="tp" style="margin:0">${esc(u.topic)}</span> <b>Part ${u.part} ・ Unit ${u.n}</b>（書 p.${u.page}）</div><div class="ut2">${esc(u.zh)} <span class="en">${esc(u.en)}</span></div>`)(UNITS[chipUnit-1]);
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="ui">${head}<div class="mb"><i style="width:${pc}%"></i></div><small>${a.length} 字${r?`（含參考詞彙 ${r}）`:''}・已熟悉 ${k}（${Math.round(pc)}%）</small></div>`;}
function renderList(){
  const q=$('#q').value.trim().toLowerCase(),f=$('#filt').value,ex=$('#showEx').checked;
  let d=vocabData.filter(v=>(chipUnit==='all'||v.unit==chipUnit)&&(!q||v.word.toLowerCase().includes(q)||v.senses.some(s=>s[1].includes(q)))&&(f==='all'||(f==='known')===known.has(v.id)));
  d=d.slice().sort(sortMode==='unit'?(a,b)=>a.unit-b.unit||a.no-b.no:(a,b)=>a.word.toLowerCase().localeCompare(b.word.toLowerCase()));
  $('#lcount').textContent=`共 ${d.length} 個單字`;renderUnitInfo();
  let html='',last=null,lp=null;
  d.forEach(v=>{
    if(sortMode==='unit'&&v.unit!==last){last=v.unit;const u=UNITS[v.unit-1];
      if(u.part!==lp){lp=u.part;html+=`<tr class="part"><td colspan="7">${POSN[u.part]}</td></tr>`;}
      html+=`<tr class="grp"><td colspan="7"><b>Unit ${u.n}</b>｜${esc(u.zh)}<span class="en">${esc(u.en)}</span><span class="tp">${esc(u.topic)}</span></td></tr>`;}
    html+=`<tr><td><span class="dot ${known.has(v.id)?'k':''}"></span></td><td class="hm ut">${sortMode==='unit'?(v.ref?'參':v.no):''}</td><td><span class="w" data-id="${v.id}">${esc(v.word)}</span>${v.ref?' <span class="rf">參考</span>':''} ${yrHTML(v)}</td><td>${v.senses.map(s=>`<div><span class="pos">${esc(s[0])}</span></div>`).join('')}</td><td>${v.senses.map(s=>`<div>${esc(s[1])}</div>`).join('')}${ex&&v.sentence?`<div class="ex2">${hl(v.sentence)}<span class="zh">${esc(v.sentZh)}</span></div>`:''}</td><td class="hm ut" title="${esc(uFull(v.unit))}">${uLabel(v.unit)}</td><td><button class="ib" data-sp="${v.id}">🔊</button></td></tr>`;});
  $('#tbody').innerHTML=html||'<tr><td colspan="7" class="empty">找不到符合的單字</td></tr>';
}
$('#tbody').addEventListener('click', e => {  if (e.target.dataset.id) openModal(+e.target.dataset.id);  if (e.target.dataset.sp) speak(vocabData[+e.target.dataset.sp].word);});/* ---------- 卡片渲染（字卡頁與彈窗共用） ---------- */function fillCard(v,ids,reverse){
  const tag=`<div class="tag">${esc(uFull(v.unit))}${v.ref?' ・ 參考詞彙':' ・ #'+v.no}</div>`,y=yrHTML(v);
  const front=`${tag}<div class="big">${esc(v.word)}</div><div style="margin-top:10px">${posHTML(v)}</div>${y?`<div style="margin-top:8px">${y}</div>`:''}<button class="btn ghost" style="margin-top:12px;padding:6px 16px" data-spk="${v.id}">🔊 單字發音</button><div class="hint">點擊卡片翻面</div>`;
  const rfront=`${tag}<div class="mean" style="font-size:1.5rem">${v.senses.map(s=>esc(s[1])).join('<br>')}</div><div>${posHTML(v)}</div><div class="hint">點擊卡片翻面</div>`;
  const back=`${tag}<div class="bk"><div class="bw">${esc(v.word)} <button class="ib" data-spk="${v.id}">🔊</button> ${y}</div>${senseHTML(v)}${extraHTML(v)}${v.sentence?`<button class="btn ghost" style="margin-top:8px;padding:5px 14px" data-sent="${v.id}">🔊 例句</button>`:''}</div>`;
  $(ids[0]).innerHTML=reverse?rfront:front;$(ids[1]).innerHTML=back;}
document.addEventListener('click', e => {  const t = e.target;  if (t.dataset.spk) { e.stopPropagation(); speak(vocabData[+t.dataset.spk].word); }  if (t.dataset.sent) { e.stopPropagation(); speak(vocabData[+t.dataset.sent].sentence, .85); }});/* ---------- 2. 字卡 ---------- */let deck = [], di = 0, rev = false;function buildDeck(keepId) {  const u = $('#fcUnit').value, f = $('#fcFilt').value;  deck = vocabData.filter(v => (u === 'all' || v.unit == u) && (f === 'all' || (f === 'known') === known.has(v.id)));  di = keepId != null ? Math.max(0, deck.findIndex(v => v.id === keepId)) : 0; showCard();}function showCard() {  $('#card').classList.remove('flip');  if (!deck.length) { $('#ff').innerHTML = '<div class="empty">此範圍沒有單字 </div>'; $('#fb').innerHTML = ''; $('#fcc').textContent = '0 / 0'; $('#fcbar').style.width = '0'; return; }  const v = deck[di]; fillCard(v, ['#ff', '#fb'], rev);  $('#fcc').textContent = `${di + 1} / ${deck.length}　${known.has(v.id) ? '✅ 已熟悉' : ''}`;  $('#fcbar').style.width = ((di + 1) / deck.length * 100) + '%';}const go = d => { if (!deck.length) return; di = (di + d + deck.length) % deck.length; showCard(); };$('#card').onclick = e => { if (!e.target.closest('[data-spk],[data-sent]')) $('#card').classList.toggle('flip'); };$('#fcNext').onclick = () => go(1); $('#fcPrev').onclick = () => go(-1);$('#fcSpk').onclick = () => deck.length && speak(deck[di].word);$('#fcShuf').onclick = () => { deck = shuffle(deck); di = 0; showCard(); };$('#fcFlipDir').onclick = e => { rev = !rev; e.target.textContent = '正面：' + (rev ? '中文' : '英文'); showCard(); };$('#fcKnow').onclick = () => { if (!deck.length) return; known.add(deck[di].id); saveK(); if ($('#fcFilt').value === 'unk') { const id = deck[di].id; deck.splice(di, 1); if (di >= deck.length) di = 0; showCard(); } else go(1); renderList(); };$('#fcUnk').onclick = () => { if (!deck.length) return; known.delete(deck[di].id); saveK(); go(1); renderList(); };['fcUnit', 'fcFilt'].forEach(id => $('#' + id).onchange = () => buildDeck());document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {  if (!$('#fc').classList.contains('active') || /INPUT|SELECT|TEXTAREA/.test(e.target.tagName)) return;  if (e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); $('#card').click(); }  else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') go(1); else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') go(-1);  else if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'k') $('#fcKnow').click(); else if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 'u') $('#fcUnk').click(); else if (e.key.toLowerCase() === 's') $('#fcSpk').click();});/* ---------- 彈窗字卡 ---------- */let mv = null;function openModal(id) { mv = vocabData[id]; $('#mcard').classList.remove('flip'); fillCard(mv, ['#mf', '#mb'], false); $('#ov').classList.add('on'); }$('#mcard').onclick = e => { if (!e.target.closest('[data-spk],[data-sent]')) $('#mcard').classList.toggle('flip'); };$('#mSpk').onclick = () => mv && speak(mv.word);$('#mClose').onclick = () => $('#ov').classList.remove('on');$('#ov').onclick = e => { if (e.target.id === 'ov') $('#ov').classList.remove('on'); };document.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') $('#ov').classList.remove('on'); });/* ---------- 3. 練習題庫 ---------- */let Q = [], qi = 0, qs = 0, qLog = [];function blankSentence(v){if(v.ref||v.word.includes(' '))return null;const m=v.sentence&&v.sentence.match(/\*([^*]+)\*/);if(!m||/\s/.test(m[1]))return null;let d=0;return v.sentence.replace(/\*([^*]+)\*/g,(x,a)=>d++?a:'＿＿＿＿＿');}
function distract(v, key, n = 3) {  const same = vocabData.filter(x => x.id !== v.id && x.pos === v.pos && x[key] !== v[key]);  const pool = same.length >= n ? same : vocabData.filter(x => x.id !== v.id && x[key] !== v[key]);  return shuffle(pool).slice(0, n);}function makeQ(v, mode) {  if (mode === 'mix') mode = ['sent', 'e2c', 'c2e', 'spell'][Math.random() * 4 | 0];  if (mode === 'sent' && !blankSentence(v)) mode = 'e2c';  const opts = key => shuffle([v, ...distract(v, key)]);  if (mode === 'sent') return { v, mode, text: blankSentence(v), sub: v.meaning, opts: opts('word').map(x => ({ t: x.word, ok: x.id === v.id })) };  if (mode === 'e2c') return { v, mode, text: `<b>${esc(v.word)}</b> <span class="pos">${esc(v.pos)}</span>`, html: 1, opts: opts('meaning').map(x => ({ t: x.meaning, ok: x.id === v.id })) };  if (mode === 'c2e') return { v, mode, text: `${esc(v.meaning)} <span class="pos">${esc(v.pos)}</span>`, html: 1, opts: opts('word').map(x => ({ t: x.word, ok: x.id === v.id })) };  return { v, mode: 'spell', text: `${esc(v.meaning)} <span class="pos">${esc(v.pos)}</span> <span class="count">（${v.word.replace(/[^ ]/g, '_').length} 字元，首字母 ${esc(v.word[0])}）</span>`, html: 1 };}function startQuiz(list) {  if (!list.length) return alert('這個範圍沒有題目');  const n = +$('#qNum').value, mode = $('#qMode').value;  Q = shuffle(list).slice(0, n).map(v => makeQ(v, mode)); qi = 0; qs = 0; qLog = [];  $('#qsetup').style.display = 'none'; $('#qbox').style.display = 'block'; showQ();}$('#qStart').onclick = () => { const u = $('#qUnit').value; startQuiz(vocabData.filter(v => u === 'all' || v.unit == u)); };$('#qWrongStart').onclick = () => startQuiz(vocabData.filter(v => wrong.has(v.id)));$('#qClear').onclick = () => { if (confirm('確定清除錯題本？')) { wrong.clear(); saveW(); } };function backToSetup() { $('#qbox').style.display = 'none'; $('#qsetup').style.display = 'block'; }function showQ() {  const q = Q[qi], box = $('#qbox');  const modeName = { sent: '例句填空', e2c: '英選中', c2e: '中選英', spell: '拼字' }[q.mode];  let h = `<div class="qh"><span>第 ${qi + 1} / ${Q.length} 題・${modeName}・${esc(uLabel(q.v.unit))}</span><span>得分 ${qs} <button class="btn ghost" id="qBack" style="padding:4px 12px;font-size:.85rem;margin-left:8px">⚙ 重新設定</button></span></div><div class="bar" style="max-width:none"><i style="width:${qi / Q.length * 100}%"></i></div>`;  h += `<div class="qq">${q.mode === 'sent' ? esc(q.text).replace('＿＿＿＿＿', '<span class="bl">　　　</span>') + `<div class="count" style="margin-top:6px">提示：${esc(q.sub)}</div>` : q.text}</div>`;  if (q.mode === 'spell') h += `<div class="toolbar"><input type="text" id="sp" class="grow" autocomplete="off" autocapitalize="off" spellcheck="false" placeholder="輸入英文單字後按 Enter"><button class="btn" id="spOk">送出</button></div>`;  else h += `<div class="opts">${q.opts.map((o, i) => `<button class="opt" data-i="${i}">${String.fromCharCode(65 + i)}. ${esc(o.t)}</button>`).join('')}</div>`;  h += `<div id="fbk"></div>`;  box.innerHTML = h; $('#qBack').onclick = backToSetup;  if (q.mode === 'spell') { const inp = $('#sp'); inp.focus(); let done = false;    const submit = () => { if (done) return; const val = inp.value.trim(); if (!val) { inp.focus(); inp.placeholder = '請先輸入單字再送出'; return; } done = true; answer(val.toLowerCase() === q.v.word.toLowerCase(), val); };    $('#spOk').onclick = submit;    inp.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter' && !e.isComposing) { e.preventDefault(); submit(); } }); }  else box.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach(b => b.onclick = () => { const o = q.opts[+b.dataset.i]; box.querySelectorAll('.opt').forEach((x, i) => { x.disabled = true; if (q.opts[i].ok) x.classList.add('c'); }); if (!o.ok) b.classList.add('x'); answer(o.ok, o.t); });}function answer(ok, given) {  const q = Q[qi], v = q.v; if ($('#sp')) { $('#sp').disabled = true; $('#spOk').disabled = true; }  if (ok) { qs++; wrong.delete(v.id); } else wrong.add(v.id); saveW();  qLog.push({ v, ok, given });  $('#fbk').innerHTML = `<div class="fb">${ok ? '✅ 答對了！' : '❌ 正確答案：<b>' + esc(q.mode === 'e2c' ? v.meaning : v.word) + '</b>'}<br><b>${esc(v.word)}</b> <span class="pos">${esc(v.pos)}</span> ${esc(v.meaning)}<br><span style="color:var(--mu)">${hl(v.sentence, v.word)}</span> <span class="sp" style="cursor:pointer" onclick="speak(vocabData[${v.id}].sentence,.85)">🔊</span></div>    <div class="ctrl"><button class="btn" id="qNext">${qi + 1 < Q.length ? '下一題 ▶' : '看結果'}</button></div>`;  $('#qNext').focus(); $('#qNext').onclick = () => { qi++; qi < Q.length ? showQ() : endQuiz(); };}function endQuiz() {  const pct = Math.round(qs / Q.length * 100), bad = qLog.filter(x => !x.ok);  $('#qbox').innerHTML = `<div class="score">${pct}%</div><p style="text-align:center;margin-bottom:12px">答對 ${qs} / ${Q.length} 題　${pct >= 90 ? '🏆 太強了！' : pct >= 70 ? '👍 不錯喔！' : '💪 再練幾次就會進步'}</p>    ${bad.length ? '<h4>需要加強的單字</h4>' + bad.map(x => `<div class="rv"><b>${esc(x.v.word)}</b> <span class="pos">${esc(x.v.pos)}</span> ${esc(x.v.meaning)}<br><span class="count">${hl(x.v.sentence)}</span></div>`).join('') : '<p class="empty">🎉 全部答對！</p>'}    <div class="ctrl"><button class="btn" id="again">再來一次</button><button class="btn ghost" id="back">回設定</button></div>`;  $('#again').onclick = () => $('#qStart').click(); $('#back').onclick = () => { $('#qbox').style.display = 'none'; $('#qsetup').style.display = 'block'; };}/* ---------- 4. 文意選填 ---------- */function renderCloze() {  $('#passages').innerHTML = '';  clozePassages.forEach((p, pi) => {    let t = esc(p.text).replace(/\[(\d+)\]/g, (m, i) => `<select class="cs" id="p${pi}-${i}"><option value="">${i}</option>${'ABCDEFGHIJ'.split('').map(c => `<option>${c}</option>`).join('')}</select>`);    const d = document.createElement('div'); d.className = 'panel';    d.innerHTML = `<div class="pt">${esc(p.title)}</div><div class="bank">${p.options.map(o => `<span>${esc(o)}</span>`).join('')}</div><div class="ptx">${t}</div>      <div class="ctrl" style="justify-content:flex-start"><button class="btn ok">對答案</button><button class="btn ghost">重做</button><b id="r${pi}"></b></div><div class="ans" id="e${pi}"><b>答案與解析</b><br>${esc(p.explanation)}</div>`;    const [chk, rst] = d.querySelectorAll('button');    chk.onclick = () => { let s = 0; for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) { const el = $(`#p${pi}-${i}`), ok = el.value === p.answers[i - 1]; el.classList.remove('ok', 'bad'); el.classList.add(ok ? 'ok' : 'bad'); if (ok) s += 10; }      $(`#r${pi}`).innerHTML = `得分：<span style="color:${s >= 60 ? 'var(--ok)' : 'var(--no)'}">${s} / 100</span>`; $(`#e${pi}`).style.display = 'block'; };    rst.onclick = () => { for (let i = 1; i <= 10; i++) { const el = $(`#p${pi}-${i}`); el.value = ''; el.classList.remove('ok', 'bad'); } $(`#r${pi}`).textContent = ''; $(`#e${pi}`).style.display = 'none'; };    $('#passages').appendChild(d);  });}/* ---------- 主題 ---------- */const themes = ['auto', 'light', 'dark'], tName = { auto: '🌓 自動', light: '☀️ 淺色', dark: '🌙 深色' };let theme = store.get('hf_theme', 'auto');function applyTheme() { const r = document.documentElement; theme === 'auto' ? r.removeAttribute('data-theme') : r.setAttribute('data-theme', theme); $('#themeBtn').textContent = tName[theme]; }$('#themeBtn').onclick = () => { theme = themes[(themes.indexOf(theme) + 1) % 3]; store.set('hf_theme', theme); applyTheme(); };applyTheme();/* ---------- 啟動 ---------- */renderChips(); renderList(); renderCloze(); stats();</script><footer><p>資料來源：LiveABC《英語高頻字彙完全攻略 增修擴編版》（Part 1 Basic＋Part 2 Advanced，共 36 單元）｜© 2026＠Maxine</p></footer></body></html>
